## 任务11：角色OID反查（ROLE-OWNER/NULL）

状态：已完成（2026-11-06，批次11）。实施前完整HEAD：`0b8b39a5ef940897f615ffcf69654f115c755b2c`；实现提交：`dfcbcd1302bd68bad5c1993fbe74b99b6ea65577`（`fix(postgresql): resolve stable role names by OID`）。批次7实现`5d66e1bb51a55bb0a01c6f23d8b1f8edad49e99f`经`git merge-base --is-ancestor ... HEAD`退出0核对，已阅读7–10保留证据；未将任务删除当依赖完成。依do-task-plans和git-commit技能实施，plan.md保持只读。

### 文件、依据与当前行为

- `pkg/server/pg_catalog.rs::Execution::role_name/evaluate`：复用缓存的公开`pg_role_facts`稳定OID/name，消除对数据库事实列表的依赖；NULL传播，未知OID返回PG18参考的`unknown (OID=<十进制OID>)`。已有database_role_name仍用于owner存在性验证，fallback不掩盖缺失owner。此为本任务反查唯一生产行为修复，无新身份/授权系统。
- `pkg/server/pg_catalog_query.rs::identity_scalar`：无FROM的普通名/pg_catalog限定名与OID参数调用进入现有目录绑定/求值链；不接管native FROM语句。此为真实扩展参数调用必需的局部入口，沿任务9/10模式。
- `pkg/server/pg_catalog_test.rs::cat14_11`：独立真实Domain/KV、ConcreteSessionDriver及PgService回归；两条非root reader连接与root均反查同一NOLOGIN角色，名称`cat14_11@owner'二`保留@、单引号和Unicode。真实GRANT INHERIT FALSE/SET TRUE、SET ROLE、RESET、REVOKE前后反查稳定且另一连接不受角色切换影响。未知0和4294967295匹配参考；角色DROP后旧OID为fallback，重建分配不同OID。同步修改已有catalog_database_description_semantics的未知123断言，不弱化其他断言。
- `pkg/server/pg_catalog.rs.md`、`pg_catalog_query.rs.md`及`docs/postgresql-18-core-compatibility-roadmap.md`：记录当前来源、fallback、局部入口及普通SQL工厂/对象owner边界。已有AsterSQL版权标记保留。

Q与扩展Parse/Describe/Bind/Execute/Close/Sync均实测，输入OID26文本/二进制、包含NULL，输出name/19、typlen64、typmod=-1、来源OID/属性号0、format0/1；二进制name为未补零UTF8字节，NULL长度-1。非法oid文本22P02，事务失败E，后续25P02，ROLLBACK恢复I且NULL查询成功。测试无静默skip、无新feature/target，清理自有数据库/角色/服务并验证监听关闭。

### 可复现脚本 cat14-11-stage.sh

SHA-256 `1ebfcfa7157cd0271ec096ebf1df191baa25159e73a28ebc5ff92eb1146e23e2`；复现时写到上表相同临时路径。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -eu
mkdir -p target/rust-slot-locks
TASK_SLOT=''
while test -z "$TASK_SLOT"; do
 for n in {1..10}; do if mkdir target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$n.lock 2>/dev/null; then TASK_SLOT=$n; break; fi; done
 if test -z "$TASK_SLOT"; then sleep 5; fi
done
export CARGO_TARGET_DIR="$PWD/target/rust-slot-$TASK_SLOT"
echo "plan=2026-10-29-catalog task=11 session=01a1b063 pid=$$" > "target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$TASK_SLOT.lock/owner"
trap 'rm "target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$TASK_SLOT.lock/owner"; rmdir "target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$TASK_SLOT.lock"' EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
echo "slot=$TASK_SLOT CARGO_TARGET_DIR=$CARGO_TARGET_DIR"
cargo fmt --all
cargo test -p astersql-server --lib pg_catalog_test::cat14_11 -- --exact --nocapture --test-threads=1
if test "${1:-}" = ready; then
 cargo test -p astersql-server --lib pg_catalog_test::cat14_10 -- --exact --nocapture --test-threads=1
 cargo test -p astersql-server --lib catalog_database_description_semantics -- --nocapture --test-threads=1
 cargo fmt --all -- --check
 make lint
 git diff --check
fi
```

### 可复现脚本 cat14-11-reference.sh

SHA-256 `0da3ace07eae5919adf8eba7886bf39757eed909bd97ee7751c4a9f724873b44`；复现时写到上表相同临时路径。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
bin=$1
tmp=$(mktemp -d /tmp/cat14-11-reference.XXXXXX)
cleanup() {
 if [ -f "$tmp/data/postmaster.pid" ]; then "$bin/pg_ctl" -D "$tmp/data" -m fast -w stop >/dev/null; fi
 test ! -f "$tmp/data/postmaster.pid"
 rm -rf "$tmp"
 echo 'cleanup: own reference cluster stopped and removed'
}
trap cleanup EXIT
"$bin/postgres" --version
"$bin/pg_config"
"$bin/psql" --version
python3 --version
"$bin/initdb" -D "$tmp/data" --no-locale --encoding=UTF8 --auth-local=trust --auth-host=reject > "$tmp/initdb.log"
"$bin/pg_ctl" -D "$tmp/data" -o "-k $tmp -h '' -p 56851" -l "$tmp/server.log" -w start >/dev/null
export REFERENCE_BIN="$bin"
export PGHOST="$tmp" PGPORT=56851 PGDATABASE=postgres
"$bin/psql" -X -P null=NULL -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 <<'SQL'
SELECT version(); SHOW server_version_num; SHOW server_encoding;
CREATE ROLE cat14_11_reader LOGIN;
CREATE ROLE "cat14_11@owner'二" NOLOGIN;
SQL
```

### 可复现脚本 cat14-11-reference-wire.py

SHA-256 `48ef8ba821cc44f2487825b305b2308dd3961bc9ace7f66c6976cac8b1ffc303`；复现时写到上表相同临时路径。

```python
import socket,struct,os,json
s=socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM)
s.connect(os.path.join(os.environ['PGHOST'],'.s.PGSQL.'+os.environ['PGPORT']))
def exact(n):
 b=b''
 while len(b)<n:
  part=s.recv(n-len(b))
  if not part: raise RuntimeError('EOF')
  b+=part
 return b
def read():
 tag=exact(1).decode();n=struct.unpack('!I',exact(4))[0];return tag,exact(n-4)
def send(tag,b):s.sendall(tag.encode()+struct.pack('!I',len(b)+4)+b)
def ready():
 out=[]
 while True:
  msg=read();out.append(msg)
  if msg[0]=='Z':return out
body=struct.pack('!I',196608)+"user\0cat14_11_reader\0database\0postgres\0\0".encode()
s.sendall(struct.pack('!I',len(body)+4)+body)
assert not any(t=='E' for t,b in ready())
def describe(b):
 n=struct.unpack('!h',b[:2])[0];out=[];i=2
 for _ in range(n):
  end=b.index(0,i);name=b[i:end].decode();i=end+1
  fields=struct.unpack('!IhIhih',b[i:i+18]);i+=18
  out.append(dict(zip(['name','source_oid','attribute','oid','typlen','typmod','format'],[name,*fields])))
 return out
def values(b):
 n=struct.unpack('!h',b[:2])[0];out=[];i=2
 for _ in range(n):
  size=struct.unpack('!i',b[i:i+4])[0];i+=4
  out.append(None if size<0 else b[i:i+size].hex());i+=max(size,0)
 return out

def summary(messages):
 return {'tags': ''.join(t for t,b in messages), 'descriptions':[describe(b) for t,b in messages if t=='T'], 'rows':[values(b) for t,b in messages if t=='D'], 'errors':[{part[:1].decode():part[1:].decode() for part in b.split(b'\0') if part} for t,b in messages if t=='E'], 'ready':[b.decode() for t,b in messages if t=='Z']}
def query(sql):
 send('Q',sql.encode()+b'\0');r=ready();print(sql,json.dumps(summary(r),ensure_ascii=False));return r


r=query("SELECT oid FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='cat14_11@owner''二'")
oid=int(bytes.fromhex([values(b) for t,b in r if t=='D'][0][0]))
sql=f"SELECT pg_catalog.pg_get_userbyid({oid}::oid),pg_get_userbyid(NULL::oid),pg_get_userbyid(0::oid),pg_get_userbyid(4294967295::oid)"
r=query(sql)
assert [values(b) for t,b in r if t=='D']==[["cat14_11@owner'二".encode().hex(),None,b'unknown (OID=0)'.hex(),b'unknown (OID=4294967295)'.hex()]]
send('P',b'owner_stmt\0SELECT pg_get_userbyid($1)\0'+struct.pack('!hI',1,26));send('D',b'Sowner_stmt\0');send('S',b'');print('parse',json.dumps(summary(ready())))
for binary in [0,1]:
 for target in [oid,None,0]:
  value=None if target is None else (struct.pack('!I',target) if binary else str(target).encode())
  bind=b'owner_portal\0owner_stmt\0'+struct.pack('!hhh',1,binary,1)+struct.pack('!i',-1 if value is None else len(value))+(value or b'')+struct.pack('!hh',1,binary)
  send('B',bind);send('D',b'Powner_portal\0');send('E',b'owner_portal\0'+struct.pack('!I',0));send('S',b'');r=ready()
  print('bind',json.dumps(dict(parameter=target,format=binary,response=summary(r)),ensure_ascii=False))
  assert not any(t=='E' for t,b in r)
  send('C',b'Powner_portal\0');send('S',b'');ready()
query('BEGIN');query("SELECT pg_get_userbyid('bad'::oid)");query('SELECT pg_get_userbyid(0::oid)');query('ROLLBACK');query(sql)
send('C',b'Sowner_stmt\0');send('S',b'');ready();send('X',b'');s.close()
print('wire-cleanup: statements/portals/connection closed')
```

## 任务12：数据库所有者投影（ROLE-OWNER）

状态：已完成（2026-11-06，批次12）。实施前完整HEAD：`463726173beacdfb24b153257fc6c468cd27250d`；实现提交：`1423525f272f27218b16085fe54890529a0ea40d`（`fix(postgresql): scope database owner joins to selected database`）。依赖批次11实现`dfcbcd1302bd68bad5c1993fbe74b99b6ea65577`经`git merge-base --is-ancestor dfcbcd1302bd68bad5c1993fbe74b99b6ea65577 HEAD`退出0核实，并读取任务7–11保留的角色事实、有效身份、成员和反查证据。不以编号文件删除推导完成。用户续跑授权允许不可缺少的共享局部接线；实际复用现有数据库owner KV、角色OID、认证和ALTER DATABASE工厂，无新身份/持久化/权限子系统。

### 变更与局部接线依据

- `pkg/server/pg_catalog.rs::database_name_filter/execute_rows`：仅基表pg_database且含JOIN时，在JOIN读取datdba前应用库名与文本/NULL字面量等值条件（含AND合取项、左右交换、绑定后的TypedLiteral）。保留完整provider缓存与最终WHERE；OR、其他关系列与owner表达式不下推。依据为本任务持久owner与pg_roles稳定OID关联必须可用：此前过滤合法库的JOIN仍先读取其他legacy库的owner，错误0A000。未降低目标legacy库的拒绝门禁，未扩大为通用查询优化器。
- `pkg/server/pg_catalog_test.rs::cat14_12`：独立真实Domain/KV、ConcreteSessionDriver、PgService及CatalogQuery回归；使用BootstrapRootAndNativeAccounts、grants开启的原生认证，两条真正reader连接，不用root代替reader或mock provider。原生建legacy库；PG CREATE DATABASE明确保存创建者owner；管理员为目标库两次ALTER OWNER。读跨root/两个reader一致，真实GRANT/REVOKE CONNECT不改变owner。测试清理自己的三个库/三个角色、portal/statement/连接和服务，关闭domain并检查监听端口释放。
- `pkg/server/pg_catalog.rs.md`及`docs/postgresql-18-core-compatibility-roadmap.md`：记录本次按库名JOIN与owner证据边界。没有修改外部依赖、Go、Bazel或只读plan.md；原有AsterSQL版权保留。

### 实际行为、字段及参考证据

`SELECT oid,datdba,pg_get_userbyid(datdba) FROM pg_catalog.pg_database WHERE datname='cat14_12_db'`：一行。oid为本端持久数据库ID映射且两次ALTER OWNER及reload后不变；datdba逐次等于共享角色事实中`cat14_12_group`、`cat14_12@owner'二`的持久OID；反查为各完整名称（含@、引号、Unicode），绝不取reader或登录者。JOIN pg_roles核对`d.datdba=r.oid`及rolname完全一致。PG新建`cat14_12_created`的owner确为创建者root，由另一reader读取同值；不是把legacy root当占位。

| 列 | 实际值/NULL | OID / typlen / typmod | 来源OID/属性号 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| oid | 非NULL、同一数据库在owner变更前后相同 | 26 / 4 / -1 | 1262 / 1 |
| datdba | 非NULL、当前共享owner角色OID，与pg_roles.oid相等 | 26 / 4 / -1 | 1262 / 3 |
| pg_get_userbyid | 非NULL、当前owner完整名字 | 19 / 64 / -1 | 0 / 0 |

简单查询format=0；扩展同一命名statement在修改前Parse/Describe，修改后每轮新Bind/Describe portal/Execute/Sync，参数OID=[25]，每个owner分别测试text/binary输出及三个参数（目标库、NULL、缺失库），共12组。binary两个OID为4字节BE，name为完整UTF8，format=1；text格式0值为十进制OID和完整名字。NULL/缺失库参数为零行、固定三列描述、SELECT 0，末尾I。新执行在另一reader连接也立刻看到新owner，不拿旧portal缓存充当证据。

未迁移目标库所需owner读取为EZ/0A000，无成功数据行；只读oid/name仍成功。无权reader ALTER OWNER为42501，未知新owner为42704且原owner不变。安全的反向等值/AND过滤JOIN返回同样owner；OR包含legacy不裁掉缺源对象，仍0A000；datname=NULL为零行；LEFT JOIN未匹配的owner为合法SQL NULL。事务错误后E，继续查询25P02/E，ROLLBACK后I且owner查询恢复。邻近pg13_001_contract另外断言BEGIN/T、有效查询/T及恢复/I。

PG18.4（Homebrew）及精确PG18.6均创建独立临时参考集群、同名LOGIN reader/两个NOLOGIN owner与目标库，实际采样Q、角色JOIN、reader拒绝、两次ALTER OWNER、新Bind/text/binary/NULL/空集和I/T/E恢复。每次退出0，集群和临时数据均由trap停止并删除；未触碰用户服务。完整postgres/pg_config/psql/构建和wire记录附后。动态OID仅比较本端关联，不要求跨端数值相同。本端上述来源属性与两个参考端一致；未把这里的数据库来源描述推广到其他虚拟目录。

### 红绿、Ready与Cargo槽位

首次新增测试编译成功后，描述助手错误假定来源为0而失败，已按真实pg_database的1262/1和1262/3修正；这是测试夹具修正，不计作生产红灯。随后相同JOIN输入在未修改生产代码时实际EZ/0A000，而期望合法owner行：`/tmp/cat14-12-baseline.log`退出101，1失败。局部修复后`/tmp/cat14-12-green.log`退出0，1通过；补齐边界后最终Ready `/tmp/cat14-12-ready.log`退出0，3个独立有效测试均通过，无忽略/零测试。

所有Cargo阶段通过原子mkdir顺序尝试1–10领取共享槽位；有效红绿与Ready实际使用槽位1，`CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/Users/Shared/work/dir/data/codes/astersql-tidb/target/rust-slot-1`。另一次冗余冷缓存编译实际领取槽位2（绝对`CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/Users/Shared/work/dir/data/codes/astersql-tidb/target/rust-slot-2`），主动中止退出143、0行为用例，不计通过；已先终止其自有Cargo及后代，再等待阶段退出与trap释放。阶段owner记录计划/任务/会话/PID，EXIT/INT/TERM清理自己的owner和锁，缓存保留；最终锁目录为空。没有--target-dir、本地patch、vendor或外部依赖变更。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -eu
mkdir -p target/rust-slot-locks
TASK_SLOT=''
while test -z "$TASK_SLOT"; do
 for n in {1..10}; do if mkdir target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$n.lock 2>/dev/null; then TASK_SLOT=$n; break; fi; done
 if test -z "$TASK_SLOT"; then sleep 5; fi
done
export CARGO_TARGET_DIR="$PWD/target/rust-slot-$TASK_SLOT"
echo "plan=2026-10-29-catalog task=12 session=01a1b069 pid=$$" > "target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$TASK_SLOT.lock/owner"
trap 'rm "target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$TASK_SLOT.lock/owner"; rmdir "target/rust-slot-locks/slot-$TASK_SLOT.lock"' EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
echo "slot=$TASK_SLOT CARGO_TARGET_DIR=$CARGO_TARGET_DIR"
cargo fmt --all
cargo test -p astersql-server --lib pg_catalog_test::cat14_12 -- --exact --nocapture --test-threads=1
if test "${1:-}" = ready; then
 cargo test -p astersql-server --lib pg_catalog_test::pg13_001_contract -- --exact --nocapture --test-threads=1
 cargo test -p astersql-server --lib catalog_database_description_semantics -- --nocapture --test-threads=1
 cargo fmt --all -- --check
 make lint
 git diff --check
fi
```

原始SHA-256 `0afb2673ac7b17748045c83f876b7ffaa66fc7d404c8c59a68364cd5cb577c83`；下文仅去行尾空白。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
bin=$1
tmp=$(mktemp -d /tmp/cat14-12-reference.XXXXXX)
cleanup() {
 if [ -f "$tmp/data/postmaster.pid" ]; then "$bin/pg_ctl" -D "$tmp/data" -m fast -w stop >/dev/null; fi
 test ! -f "$tmp/data/postmaster.pid"
 rm -rf "$tmp"
 echo 'cleanup: own reference cluster stopped and removed'
}
trap cleanup EXIT
"$bin/postgres" --version
"$bin/pg_config"
"$bin/psql" --version
python3 --version
"$bin/initdb" -D "$tmp/data" --no-locale --encoding=UTF8 --auth-local=trust --auth-host=reject > "$tmp/initdb.log"
"$bin/pg_ctl" -D "$tmp/data" -o "-k $tmp -h '' -p 56852" -l "$tmp/server.log" -w start >/dev/null
export REFERENCE_BIN="$bin"
export PGHOST="$tmp" PGPORT=56852 PGDATABASE=postgres
"$bin/psql" -X -P null=NULL -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 <<'SQL'
SELECT version(); SHOW server_version_num; SHOW server_encoding;
CREATE ROLE cat14_12_reader LOGIN;
CREATE ROLE "cat14_12@owner'二" NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE cat14_12_group NOLOGIN;
CREATE DATABASE cat14_12_db OWNER "cat14_12@owner'二";
SQL
python3 /tmp/cat14-12-reference-wire.py
```

### 参考端wire采样脚本

原始SHA-256 `01f6e19d6c35be3ffd3f71023ef35fed58c8a6682155b64b950d33b8b6d40e87`；下文仅去行尾空白。

```python
import socket,struct,os,json
s=socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM)
s.connect(os.path.join(os.environ['PGHOST'],'.s.PGSQL.'+os.environ['PGPORT']))
def exact(n):
 b=b''
 while len(b)<n:
  part=s.recv(n-len(b))
  if not part: raise RuntimeError('EOF')
  b+=part
 return b
def read():
 tag=exact(1).decode();n=struct.unpack('!I',exact(4))[0];return tag,exact(n-4)
def send(tag,b):s.sendall(tag.encode()+struct.pack('!I',len(b)+4)+b)
def ready():
 out=[]
 while True:
  msg=read();out.append(msg)
  if msg[0]=='Z':return out
body=struct.pack('!I',196608)+"user\0cat14_12_reader\0database\0postgres\0\0".encode()
s.sendall(struct.pack('!I',len(body)+4)+body)
assert not any(t=='E' for t,b in ready())
def describe(b):
 n=struct.unpack('!h',b[:2])[0];out=[];i=2
 for _ in range(n):
  end=b.index(0,i);name=b[i:end].decode();i=end+1
  fields=struct.unpack('!IhIhih',b[i:i+18]);i+=18
  out.append(dict(zip(['name','source_oid','attribute','oid','typlen','typmod','format'],[name,*fields])))
 return out
def values(b):
 n=struct.unpack('!h',b[:2])[0];out=[];i=2
 for _ in range(n):
  size=struct.unpack('!i',b[i:i+4])[0];i+=4
  out.append(None if size<0 else b[i:i+size].hex());i+=max(size,0)
 return out

def summary(messages):
 return {'tags': ''.join(t for t,b in messages), 'descriptions':[describe(b) for t,b in messages if t=='T'], 'rows':[values(b) for t,b in messages if t=='D'], 'errors':[{part[:1].decode():part[1:].decode() for part in b.split(b'\0') if part} for t,b in messages if t=='E'], 'ready':[b.decode() for t,b in messages if t=='Z']}
def query(sql):
 send('Q',sql.encode()+b'\0');r=ready();print(sql,json.dumps(summary(r),ensure_ascii=False));return r


import subprocess
bin=os.environ['REFERENCE_BIN']
def admin(sql):
 subprocess.run([bin+'/psql','-X','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-U',os.environ.get('USER','xiangmin'),'-c',sql],check=True)
def rows(r): return [values(b) for t,b in r if t=='D']
sql="SELECT oid,datdba,pg_get_userbyid(datdba) FROM pg_catalog.pg_database WHERE datname='cat14_12_db'"
initial=rows(query(sql))[0]
assert bytes.fromhex(initial[2]).decode()=="cat14_12@owner'二"
r=query("SELECT d.datdba,r.oid,r.rolname FROM pg_database d JOIN pg_roles r ON r.oid=d.datdba WHERE d.datname='cat14_12_db'")
assert rows(r)==[[initial[1],initial[1],initial[2]]]
r=query('ALTER DATABASE cat14_12_db OWNER TO cat14_12_reader')
assert any(t=='E' and b'C42501\0' in b for t,b in r)
send('P',b'db_owner_stmt\0SELECT oid,datdba,pg_get_userbyid(datdba) FROM pg_database WHERE datname=$1\0'+struct.pack('!hI',1,25));send('D',b'Sdb_owner_stmt\0');send('S',b'');print('parse',json.dumps(summary(ready())))
for role,name in [('cat14_12_group','cat14_12_group'), ("\"cat14_12@owner'二\"","cat14_12@owner'二")]:
 admin('ALTER DATABASE cat14_12_db OWNER TO '+role)
 current=rows(query(sql))[0];assert current[0]==initial[0] and bytes.fromhex(current[2]).decode()==name
 for binary in [0,1]:
  for target in ['cat14_12_db',None,'cat14_12_absent']:
   value=None if target is None else target.encode()
   bind=b'db_owner_portal\0db_owner_stmt\0'+struct.pack('!hh',0,1)+struct.pack('!i',-1 if value is None else len(value))+(value or b'')+struct.pack('!hh',1,binary)
   send('B',bind);send('D',b'Pdb_owner_portal\0');send('E',b'db_owner_portal\0'+struct.pack('!I',0));send('S',b'');r=ready()
   print('bind',json.dumps(dict(parameter=target,format=binary,response=summary(r)),ensure_ascii=False))
   assert not any(t=='E' for t,b in r)
   if target=='cat14_12_db':
    expected=[struct.pack('!I',int(bytes.fromhex(v))).hex() if binary else v for v in current[:2]]+[current[2]]
    assert rows(r)==[expected]
   else: assert not rows(r)
   send('C',b'Pdb_owner_portal\0');send('S',b'');ready()
query('BEGIN');query("SELECT pg_get_userbyid('bad'::oid)");query(sql);query('ROLLBACK');assert rows(query(sql))[0]==initial
send('C',b'Sdb_owner_stmt\0');send('S',b'');ready();send('X',b'');s.close()
print('wire-cleanup: statements/portals/connection closed')
```
